======================================================================
SPORTS KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Team Sports
Concept: Sports
Definition: Competitive physical activities governed by rules.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in sports.
- Widely covered in physical education and general knowledge.
- Relevant to athletes, coaches and sports enthusiasts.
Example:
Sports is an important topic in sports.
Related Concepts:
Competition, Athletics, Fitness, Teamwork, Rules, Championships

Entry 2
Category: Team Sports
Concept: Football
Definition: A team sport played between two teams aiming to score goals.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in sports.
- Widely covered in physical education and general knowledge.
- Relevant to athletes, coaches and sports enthusiasts.
Example:
The FIFA World Cup is football's most prestigious tournament.
